  169 | async function assetFromUri(
  170 |   uri: string,
  171 |   kind: 'IMAGE' | 'AUDIO',
  172 |   name: string,
  173 |   mimeType: string | null,
  174 | ): Promise<PortableAsset | null> {
  175 |   try {
  176 |     if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
  177 |       const response = await fetch(
  178 |       // portableWebAudioHydrationV2
  179 |       Platform.OS === 'web' &&
  180 |       uri.startsWith('ljingle-web-audio:')
  181 |         ? await hydrateWebAudio(uri)
  182 |         : uri,
  183 |     );
  184 |       const blob = await response.blob();
  185 |       // portableFileReaderBase64V1
  186 |       // Browser-native async conversion avoids freezing the JS thread on
  187 |       // large audio files.
  188 |       const base64 =
  189 |         await new Promise<string | null>(
  190 |           (resolve, reject) => {
  191 |             const reader = new FileReader();
  192 | 
  193 |             reader.onerror = () =>
  194 |               reject(
  195 |                 reader.error ||
  196 |                   new Error(
  197 |                     'Browser could not read portable asset.',
  198 |                   ),
  199 |               );
  200 | 
  201 |             reader.onload = () => {
  202 |               const result =
  203 |                 typeof reader.result === 'string'
  204 |                   ? reader.result
  205 |                   : '';
  206 | 
  207 |               const comma =
  208 |                 result.indexOf(',');
  209 | 
  210 |               resolve(
  211 |                 comma >= 0
  212 |                   ? result.slice(comma + 1)
  213 |                   : null,
  214 |               );
  215 |             };
  216 | 
  217 |             reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
  218 |           },
  219 |         );
  220 | 
  221 |       const byteLength = blob.size;
  222 | 
  223 |       return {
  224 |         assetId: id('asset'),
  225 |         kind,
  226 |         name,
  227 |         mimeType: mimeType || blob.type || null,
  228 |         byteLength,
  229 |         base64,

  552 |   );
  553 | 
  554 |   if (!entry) {
  555 |     throw new Error('Zapis ne postoji.');
  556 |   }

  581 |       {
  582 |         track_id: row.track_id,
  583 |         title: row.track_title,

  586 |         original_name: row.original_name,

  589 |         created_at: row.track_created_at,
  590 |         modified_at: row.track_modified_at,
  591 |       },
  592 |     );
  593 | 
  594 |     pack.objects.push(frequency);
  595 |     await attachFrequencyAudio(
  596 |       frequency,
  597 |       pack.assets,
  598 |     );
  599 | 
  600 |     pack.relationships.push({
  601 |       sourcePortableId: entry.portableId,

  603 |         frequency.portableId,

  868 | async function webPortableShareChoice(
  869 |   pack: LjinglePortablePackage,
  870 | ) {
  871 |   // portableWebDialogV1
  872 |   // Use the browser top-layer <dialog> instead of a hand-made fixed overlay.
  873 |   // This cannot disappear behind React Navigation's aria-hidden screens.
  874 |   if (
  875 |     typeof document === 'undefined'
  876 |   ) {
  877 |     throw new Error(
  878 |       'Web share menu nije dostupan.',
  879 |     );
  880 |   }
  881 | 
  882 |   return new Promise<string | null>(
  883 |     (resolve) => {
  884 |       const dialog =
  885 |         document.createElement('dialog');
  886 | 
  887 |       dialog.setAttribute(
  888 |         'aria-label',
  889 |         'Share from LJingle',
  890 |       );
  891 | 
  892 |       dialog.style.width =
  893 |         'min(480px, calc(100vw - 32px))';
  894 |       dialog.style.maxWidth = '480px';
  895 |       dialog.style.border =
  896 |         '1px solid rgba(30,30,30,0.14)';
  897 |       dialog.style.borderRadius = '22px';
  898 |       dialog.style.padding = '0';
  899 |       dialog.style.background = '#F2EEE5';
  900 |       dialog.style.color = '#111111';
  901 |       dialog.style.boxShadow =
  902 |         '0 24px 90px rgba(0,0,0,0.28)';
  903 |       dialog.style.fontFamily =
  904 |         'Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif';
  905 | 
  906 |       const shell =
  907 |         document.createElement('div');
  908 | 
  909 |       shell.style.padding = '20px';
  910 | 
  911 |       const kicker =
  912 |         document.createElement('div');
  913 | 
  914 |       kicker.textContent =
  915 |         'SHARE FROM LJINGLE';
  916 |       kicker.style.fontSize = '10px';
  917 |       kicker.style.fontWeight = '800';
  918 |       kicker.style.letterSpacing = '1.4px';
  919 |       kicker.style.opacity = '0.55';
  920 | 
  921 |       const title =
  922 |         document.createElement('div');
  923 | 
  924 |       title.textContent = pack.title;
  925 |       title.style.marginTop = '7px';
  926 |       title.style.fontFamily =
  927 |         'Georgia, Times New Roman, serif';
  928 |       title.style.fontSize = '24px';

 1068 | async function choosePortableShareUnlocked(
 1069 |   pack: LjinglePortablePackage,
 1070 | ) {
 1071 |   let choice: string | null = null;
 1072 | 
 1073 |   if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
 1074 |     choice =
 1075 |       await webPortableShareChoice(
 1076 |         pack,
 1077 |       );
 1078 |   } else {
 1079 |     choice =
 1080 |       await new Promise<string | null>(
 1081 |         (resolve) => {
 1082 |           Alert.alert(
 1083 |             'Share from LJingle',
 1084 |             pack.title,
 1085 |             [
 1086 |               {
 1087 |                 text: 'Visual card',
 1088 |                 onPress: () =>
 1089 |                   resolve('visual'),
 1090 |               },
 1091 |               {
 1092 |                 text: 'Bridge',
 1093 |                 onPress: () =>
 1094 |                   resolve('bridge'),
 1095 |               },
 1096 |               {
 1097 |                 text: '.ljingle',
 1098 |                 onPress: () =>
 1099 |                   resolve('portable'),
 1100 |               },
 1101 |               {
 1102 |                 text: 'Cancel',
 1103 |                 style: 'cancel',
 1104 |                 onPress: () =>
 1105 |                   resolve(null),
 1106 |               },
 1107 |             ],
 1108 |             {
 1109 |               cancelable: true,
 1110 |               onDismiss: () =>
 1111 |                 resolve(null),
 1112 |             },
 1113 |           );
 1114 |         },
 1115 |       );
 1116 |   }
 1117 | 
 1118 |   if (choice === 'visual') {
 1119 |     return sharePortableVisualCard(
 1120 |       pack,
 1121 |     );
 1122 |   }
 1123 | 
 1124 |   if (choice === 'bridge') {
 1125 |     const sent =
 1126 |       await sendPackageToSavedBridge(
 1127 |         pack,
 1128 |       );

 1131 |       'Sent through Bridge',
 1132 |       'Delivered to ' +
 1133 |         sent.toBridgeId +
 1134 |         '.',
 1135 |     );
 1136 | 
 1137 |     return sent.messageId;
 1138 |   }
 1139 | 
 1140 |   if (choice === 'portable') {
 1141 |     return sharePackage(pack);
 1142 |   }
 1143 | 
 1144 |   return null;
 1145 | }

 1147 | let portableShareInFlight = false;
 1148 | 
 1149 | async function choosePortableShare(
 1150 |   pack: LjinglePortablePackage,
 1151 | ) {
 1152 |   if (portableShareInFlight) {
 1153 |     return null;
 1154 |   }
 1155 | 
 1156 |   portableShareInFlight = true;
 1157 | 
 1158 |   try {
 1159 |     return await choosePortableShareUnlocked(
 1160 |       pack,
 1161 |     );
 1162 |   } finally {
 1163 |     portableShareInFlight = false;
 1164 |   }
 1165 | }

 1167 | async function sharePackage(
 1168 |   pack: LjinglePortablePackage,
 1169 | ) {
 1170 |   const filename =
 1171 |     safeName(pack.title) + '.ljingle';
 1172 | 
 1173 |   const text = JSON.stringify(
 1174 |     pack,
 1175 |     null,
 1176 |     2,
 1177 |   );
 1178 | 
 1179 |   if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
 1180 |     const web = globalThis as unknown as {
 1181 |       Blob?: typeof Blob;
 1182 |       URL?: typeof URL;
 1183 |       document?: Document;
 1184 |     };
 1185 | 
 1186 |     if (
 1187 |       !web.Blob ||
 1188 |       !web.URL ||
 1189 |       !web.document
 1190 |     ) {
 1191 |       throw new Error(
 1192 |         'Web download nije dostupan.',
 1193 |       );
 1194 |     }
 1195 | 
 1196 |     const blob = new web.Blob(
 1197 |       [text],
 1198 |       {
 1199 |         type:
 1200 |           'application/x-ljingle+json',
 1201 |       },
 1202 |     );
 1203 | 
 1204 |     const href =
 1205 |       web.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
 1206 | 
 1207 |     const anchor =
 1208 |       web.document.createElement('a');
 1209 |

 1174 |     pack,
 1175 |     null,
 1176 |     2,
 1177 |   );
 1178 | 
 1179 |   if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
 1180 |     const web = globalThis as unknown as {
 1181 |       Blob?: typeof Blob;
 1182 |       URL?: typeof URL;
 1183 |       document?: Document;
 1184 |     };
 1185 | 
 1186 |     if (
 1187 |       !web.Blob ||

 1189 |       !web.document
 1190 |     ) {

 1192 |         'Web download nije dostupan.',

  176 |   const remove = async (itemId: string) => {
  177 |     if (busyId || !collection) return;
  178 |     setBusyId(itemId);
  179 |     setError(null);
  180 |     try {
  181 |       await collectionApi.removeItem(collectionId, itemId);
  182 |       setCollection({ ...collection, items: collection.items.filter((item) => item.itemId !== itemId) });
  183 |     } catch (caught) {
  184 |       setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'Stavka nije uklonjena.');
  185 |     } finally {
  186 |       setBusyId(null);
  187 |     }
  188 |   };
  189 | 
  190 |   const removeCollection = async () => {
  191 |     if (deleting) return;
  192 |     setDeleting(true);
  193 |     try {
  194 |       await collectionApi.remove(collectionId);
  195 |       deletedFeedback();
  196 |       navigation.goBack();
  197 |     } catch (caught) {
  198 |       setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'Kolekcija nije obrisana.');
  199 |     } finally {
  200 |       setDeleting(false);
  201 |     }
  202 |   };
  203 | 
  204 |   const memberKeys = new Set(collection?.items.map((item) => `${item.entityType}:${item.entityId}`) ?? []);
  205 |

  208 |   }

  210 |   if (!collection) {
  211 |     return <SafeAreaView style={styles.center}><Text style={styles.errorText}>{error ?? 'Kolekcija nije pronađena.'}</Text></SafeAreaView>;
  212 |   }
